<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَبْدٍ الْقَارِيِّ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ مَا بَالُ رِجَالٍ يَنْحَلُونَ أَبْنَاءَهُمْ نُحْلاً ثُمَّ يُمْسِكُونَهَا فَإِنْ مَاتَ ابْنُ أَحَدِهِمْ قَالَ مَالِي بِيَدِي لَمْ أُعْطِهِ أَحَدًا ‏.‏ وَإِنْ مَاتَ هُوَ قَالَ هُوَ لاِبْنِي قَدْ كُنْتُ أَعْطَيْتُهُ إِيَّاهُ ‏.‏ مَنْ نَحَلَ نِحْلَةً فَلَمْ يَحُزْهَا الَّذِي نُحِلَهَا - حَتَّى يَكُونَ إِنْ مَاتَ لِوَرَثَتِهِ - فَهِيَ بَاطِلٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Umar&#x27;s attributed statement about gifts retained by the giver is preserved as a Companion report, not Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az-Zubayr from Abd ar-Rahman ibn Abd al-Qari that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;What is wrong with men who give their sons gifts and then keep them and if the son dies, they say, &#x27;My property is in my possession and I did not give it to anyone.&#x27; But if they themselves are dying, they say, &#x27;It belongs to my son, I gave it to him.&#x27; Whoever gives a gift, and does not hand it over to the one to whom it was given, the gift is invalid, and if he dies it belongs to the heirs in general</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1441]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने उरवा से, उन्होंने अब्दुर रहमान इब्न अब्द अल-क़ारी से रिवायत की के उमर इब्न अल-ख़त्ताब (रज़ि.) ने फ़रमाया: उन लोगों का क्या हाल है जो अपने बेटों को तोहफ़ा देते हैं और फिर उसे वापस ले लेते हैं, और मरते वक़्त कहते हैं के ये मेरा माल है मैं ने किसी को नहीं दिया। जो शख़्स तोहफ़ा दे और उसे देने वाले के हवाले न करे तो वो तोहफ़ा बातिल है और मरने के बाद वो वारिसीन में शामिल होगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے عروہ سے، انہوں نے عبدالرحمن بن عبد القاری سے روایت کی کہ عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ نے فرمایا: ان لوگوں کا کیا حال ہے جو اپنے بیٹوں کو تحفہ دیتے ہیں اور پھر اسے واپس لے لیتے ہیں، اور مرتے وقت کہتے ہیں کہ یہ میرا مال ہے میں نے کسی کو نہیں دیا۔ جو شخص تحفہ دے اور اسے دینے والے کے حوالے نہ کرے تو وہ تحفہ باطل ہے اور مرنے کے بعد وہ ورثاء میں شامل ہوگا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Ibn Shihab se, unhon ne Urwa se, unhon ne Abdur Rahman ibn Abd al-Qari se riwayat ki ke Umar ibn al-Khattab (R.A) ne farmaya: Un logon ka kya haal hai jo apne beton ko tohfa dete hain aur phir use wapas le lete hain, aur marte waqt kehte hain ke ye mera maal hai main ne kisi ko nahi diya. Jo shakhs tohfa de aur use dene wale ke hawale na kare to wo tohfa batil hai aur marne ke baad wo warisheen mein shamil hoga.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1441" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1427

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1442)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1442</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1442 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، عَنْ أَبِي غَطَفَانَ بْنِ طَرِيفٍ الْمُرِّيِّ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ مَنْ وَهَبَ هِبَةً لِصِلَةِ رَحِمٍ أَوْ عَلَى وَجْهِ صَدَقَةٍ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَرْجِعُ فِيهَا وَمَنْ وَهَبَ هِبَةً يَرَى أَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا أَرَادَ بِهَا الثَّوَابَ فَهُوَ عَلَى هِبَتِهِ يَرْجِعُ فِيهَا إِذَا لَمْ يُرْضَ مِنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الْهِبَةَ إِذَا تَغَيَّرَتْ عِنْدَ الْمَوْهُوبِ لَهُ لِلثَّوَابِ بِزِيَادَةٍ أَوْ نُقْصَانٍ فَإِنَّ عَلَى الْمَوْهُوبِ لَهُ أَنْ يُعْطِيَ صَاحِبَهَا قِيمَتَهَا يَوْمَ قَبَضَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Select the separately transmitted Umar report embedded between Malik&#x27;s legal explanations. The preceding and following explanations remain source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn from Abu Ghatafan ibn Tarif al-Muriyi that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;If someone gives a gift to strengthen ties with a relative or as sadaqa, he cannot have it returned. If some one, however, gives a gift seeking by it favour or reward, he has his gift and can reclaim it if he does not have satisfaction from it.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1442]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1442</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1442 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about some one who gives a gift not intending a reward is that he calls witnesses to it. It is affirmed for the one to whom it has been given unless the giver dies before the one to whom it was given receives the gift.&quot; He said, &quot;If the giver wants to keep the gift after he has had it witnessed, he cannot. If the recipient claims it from him, he takes it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If some one gives a gift and then withdraws it and the recipient brings a witness to testify for him that he was given the gift, be it goods, gold, silver or animals, the recipient is made to take an oath. If he refuses, the giver is made to take an oath. If he also refuses to take an oath, he gives to the recipient what he claims from him if he has at least one witness. If he does not have a witness, he has nothing . &quot; Malik said, &quot;If someone gives a gift not expecting anything in return and then the recipient dies, the heirs are in his place. If the giver dies before the recipient has received his gift, the recipient has nothing. That is because he was given a gift which he did not take possession of. If the giver wants to keep it, and he has called witnesses to the gift, he cannot do that. If the recipient claims his right he takes it.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community is that if the gift is returned to the one who gave it for recompense, and its value has been either increased or decreased, the one to whom it has been given gives the owner its value on the day he received it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے امام مالک کو کہتے سنا کہ ہمارے ہاں جو شخص تحفہ دے تو اس پر گواہ بنا لے، تب ہی وہ تحفہ پانے والے کا ہو گا، الا یہ کہ دینے والا مر جائے۔ اگر دینے والا گواہ بنانے کے بعد اسے واپس لینا چاہے تو نہیں لے سکتا۔</p>

## Narration 1428

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1443)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا رَجُلٍ أُعْمِرَ عُمْرَى لَهُ وَلِعَقِبِهِ فَإِنَّهَا لِلَّذِي يُعْطَاهَا لاَ تَرْجِعُ إِلَى الَّذِي أَعْطَاهَا أَبَدًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لأَنَّهُ أَعْطَى عَطَاءً وَقَعَتْ فِيهِ الْمَوَارِيثُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1443</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1443 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute, is that if a man gives sadaqa to his son - sadaqa which the son takes possession of or which is in the father&#x27;s keeping and the father has had his sadaqa witnessed, he cannot take back any of it because he cannot reclaim any sadaqa.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community in the case of someone who gives his son a gift or grants him a gift which is not sadaqa is that he can take it back as long as the child does not start a debt, which people claim from him, and which they trust him for on the strength of the gift his father has given him. The father cannot take back anything from the gift after debts are started against it. &quot;If a man gives his son or daughter something and a woman marries the man, and she only marries him for the wealth and the property which his father has given him and so the father wants to take that back, or, if a man marries a woman whose father has given her a gift and he marries her with an increased bride-price because of the wealth and property that her father has given, then the father says, &#x27;I will take that back,&#x27; then the father cannot take back any of that from the son or daughter if it is as I have described to you.&quot; Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman ibn Awf from Jabir ibn Abdullah al-Ansari that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If someone is given a life pension, for him and his posterity, it belongs to the person to whom it has been given. It never reverts to the one who gave it because he gave a gift and the rules of inheritance apply to it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह अंसारी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, जो शख्स किसी को उमरा (तोहफे के तौर पर जिंदगी भर के लिए देना) दे, उसके वास्ते और उसके वारिसों के वास्ते, तोह फिर वोह उमरा उसी का हो जाता है, देने वाले को फिर नहीं मिल सकता।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<p dir="rtl">جابر بن عبداللہ انصاری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص کسی کو عمریٰ دے اس کے واسطے اور اس کے وارثوں کے واسطے تو پھر وہ عمرہ اس کا ہو جاتا ہے دینے ولاے کو پھر نہیں مل سکتا ۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir bin Abdullah Ansari (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, jo shakhs kisi ko Umra (tohfe ke taur par zindagi bhar ke liye dena) de, uske waste aur uske warison ke waste, toh phir woh umra usi ka ho jata hai, dene wale ko phir nahi mil sakta.</p>

## Narration 1429

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1444)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1444]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1444" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ مَكْحُولاً الدِّمَشْقِيَّ، يَسْأَلُ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ عَنِ الْعُمْرَى، وَمَا يَقُولُ النَّاسُ فِيهَا فَقَالَ الْقَاسِمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ مَا أَدْرَكْتُ النَّاسَ إِلاَّ وَهُمْ عَلَى شُرُوطِهِمْ فِي أَمْوَالِهِمْ وَفِيمَا أُعْطُوا ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الْعُمْرَى تَرْجِعُ إِلَى الَّذِي أَعْمَرَهَا إِذَا لَمْ يَقُلْ هِيَ لَكَ وَلِعَقِبِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1444" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor). Makhul&#x27;s question and al-Qasim&#x27;s reply are a Successor-era report. Malik&#x27;s following statement of community practice is kept as commentary. Speaker evidence points to the directly named person asked, not the same name embedded in a transmitter&#x27;s patronymic.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said that Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim ibn Muhammad heard Makhul ad-Dimashqi ask al-Qasim ibn Muhammad about the life pension and what people said about it. Al- Qasim ibn Muhammad said, &quot;I have only come upon people who keep to the conditions they make about their property and what they are given.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1444]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1444" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community is that the life pension reverts to the one who makes it a life pension unless he says, &#x27;It belongs to you and your posterity</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1444" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1444</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1444 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर्रहमान बिन कासिम ने सुना मखूल से, जो कासिम से पूछ रहे थे उमरा के मुतालिक के लोगों का क्या कौल है इस में? कासिम ने कहा, मैं ने तोह लोगों को अपनी शर्तें पूरी करते हुए पाया अपने मालों में और जो कुछ वोह दिया रखते थे उसको भी पूरा करते थे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1444" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1430

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1445)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1445]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1445" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1445</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1445 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، وَرِثَ مِنْ حَفْصَةَ بِنْتِ عُمَرَ دَارَهَا قَالَ وَكَانَتْ حَفْصَةُ قَدْ أَسْكَنَتْ بِنْتَ زَيْدِ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ مَا عَاشَتْ فَلَمَّا تُوُفِّيَتْ بِنْتُ زَيْدٍ قَبَضَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ الْمَسْكَنَ وَرَأَى أَنَّهُ لَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1445" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). Nafi&#x27;s report of Abdullah ibn Umar taking possession of Hafsa&#x27;s dwelling after the permitted occupant died. This describes Companion conduct, with no Prophetic attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar inherited the house of Hafsa bint Umar. He said, &quot;Hafsa gave lodging to the daughter of Zayd ibn al-Khattab for as long as she lived. When the daughter of Zayd died, Abdullah ibn Umar took possession of the dwelling and considered that it was his</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1445]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1445" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

## Narration 1431

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1446)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ، مَوْلَى الْمُنْبَعِثِ عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهُ عَنِ اللُّقَطَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ اعْرِفْ عِفَاصَهَا وَوِكَاءَهَا ثُمَّ عَرِّفْهَا سَنَةً فَإِنْ جَاءَ صَاحِبُهَا وَإِلاَّ فَشَأْنَكَ بِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَضَالَّةُ الْغَنَمِ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ هِيَ لَكَ أَوْ لأَخِيكَ أَوْ لِلذِّئْبِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَضَالَّةُ الإِبِلِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَا لَكَ وَلَهَا مَعَهَا سِقَاؤُهَا وَحِذَاؤُهَا تَرِدُ الْمَاءَ وَتَأْكُلُ الشَّجَرَ حَتَّى يَلْقَاهَا رَبُّهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1446</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1446 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman from Yazid, the mawla of al-Munbaith that Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani said, &quot;A man came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and asked him about finds. He said, &#x27;Memorize the characteristics of the object found, then publicise it for a year. If the owner comes, give it to him. If not, then it is your business.&#x27; He said, &#x27;What about lost sheep, Messenger of Allah?&#x27; He said, &#x27;They are yours, your brother&#x27;s or the wolf&#x27;s.&#x27; He said, &#x27;And the lost camel?&#x27; He said, &#x27;It&#x27;s none of your concern. It has its water and its feet. It will reach water and eat trees until its owner finds it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1446</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1446 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जैद बिन खालिद जुहानी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के एक शख्स आया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास और पूछा आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से लुक्ता (गिरी हुई चीज़) के बारे में। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, पहचान रख तरफ उसके लुक्ते की, ख्वाह चमड़े में हो या कपड़े में, और पहचान रख बंधन उसका, फिर एक बरस तक लोगों से उसका हाल कहा कर, अगर उसका मालिक मिल जाए तोह उसके दे दे, नहीं तोह ले ले। फिर उसने कहा, अगर कोई बकरी बहकी भटकी मिल जाए या रसूल अल्लाह? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, बकरी तेरे काम में आएगी या तेरे भाई के, नहीं तोह भेड़िया खा जाएगा। फिर उस शख्स ने कहा, अगर ऊंट भूला मिले? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, ऊंट से तुझे क्या काम? वोह तोह अपने साथ अपना पानी रखता है और मौज़े (पैर) रखता है, जहां उसको पानी मिल जाता है पी लेता है, जो दरख्त मिलता है खा लेता है यहां तक के मालिक उसका उसको पा लेता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص آیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس اور پوچھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے لقطہ کو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پہچان رکھ طرف اس کا لقطہ ہو خواہ چمڑے میں ہو یا کپڑے میں ہو پاور پہچان رکھ بندھن اس کا پھر ایک برس تک لوگوں سے اس کا حال کہا کر اگر اس کا مالک مل جائے تو اس کے دے دے نہیں تو لے لے پھر اس نے کہا گر کوئی بکری بہکی بھٹکی مل جائے یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا بکری تیرے کام میں آئے گی یا تیرے بھائی کے نہیں تو بھیڑیا کھا جائے گا پھر اس شخص نے کہا اگر اونٹ بھولا ملے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اونٹ سے تجھے کیا کام وہ تو اپنے ساتھ اپنا پانی رکھتا ہے اور موزے رکھتا ہے جہاں اس کو پانی مل جاتا ہے پی لیتا ہے جو درخت ملتا ہے کھالیتا ہے یہاں تک کہ مالک اس کا اس کو پا لیاتا ہے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Khalid Juhani (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs aaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aur pucha Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se luqta (giri hui cheez) ke bare mein. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, pehchan rakh taraf uske luqte ki, khwah chamde mein ho ya kapde mein, aur pehchan rakh bandhan uska, phir ek baras tak logon se uska haal kaha kar, agar uska malik mil jaye toh uske de de, nahi toh le le. Phir usne kaha, agar koi bakri behki bhatki mil jaye ya Rasool Allah? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, bakri tere kaam mein aayegi ya tere bhai ke, nahi toh bhediya kha jayega. Phir us shakhs ne kaha, agar oonth bhoola mile? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, oonth se tujhe kya kaam? Woh toh apne saath apna pani rakhta hai aur mouze (pair) rakhta hai, jahan usko pani mil jata hai pi leta hai, jo darakht milta hai kha leta hai yahan tak ke malik uska usko pa leta hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1432

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1447)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ مُوسَى، عَنْ مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ بَدْرٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّ أَبَاهُ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، نَزَلَ مَنْزِلَ قَوْمٍ بِطَرِيقِ الشَّامِ فَوَجَدَ صُرَّةً فِيهَا ثَمَانُونَ دِينَارًا فَذَكَرَهَا لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ عَرِّفْهَا عَلَى أَبْوَابِ الْمَسَاجِدِ وَاذْكُرْهَا لِكُلِّ مَنْ يَأْتِي مِنَ الشَّأْمِ سَنَةً فَإِذَا مَضَتِ السَّنَةُ فَشَأْنَكَ بِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1447" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source reports a found purse and Umar&#x27;s instruction to announce it. Preserve the exact account and amount without supplying any missing parallel.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ayyub ibn Musa from Muawiya ibn Abdullah ibn Badr al-Juhani that his father informed him that he stopped with a people on the way to Syria and he found a purse which had eighty dinars in it. He mentioned that to Umar ibn al-Khattab. Umar said to him, &quot;Announce it at the doors of the mosques and mention it to everyone who comes from Syria for a year. When a year passes, it is your business</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1447]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1447" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुआविया बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन बद्र अल-जुहानी (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) से रिवायत है, उनके बाप ने बयान किया के उन्होंने शाम के रास्ते में एक मंज़िल में, जहां लोग उतर चुके थे, एक थैली पायी जिस में अस्सी (80) दीनार थे। उन्होंने हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से बयान किया, आपने कहा मस्जिदों के दरवाज़ों पर लोगों से कहा कर और जो शख्स शाम से आए उससे बयान किया कर, एक बरस तक। जब एक बरस गुज़र जाए फिर तुझ को इख्तियार है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1447" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muawiya bin Abdullah bin Badr Al-Juhani (Rahmatullah Alaih) se riwayat hai, unke baap ne bayan kiya ke unhone Shaam ke raste mein ek manzil mein, jahan log utar chuke the, ek thaili payi jis mein assi (80) dinar the. Unhone Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se bayan kiya, aapne kaha masjidon ke darwazon par logon se kaha kar aur jo shakhs Shaam se aaye usse bayan kiya kar, ek baras tak. Jab ek baras guzar jaye phir tujh ko ikhtiyar hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1447" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1433

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1448)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1448]</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1448</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1448 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، وَجَدَ لُقَطَةً فَجَاءَ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَقَالَ لَهُ إِنِّي وَجَدْتُ لُقَطَةً فَمَاذَا تَرَى فِيهَا فَقَالَ لَهُ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ عَرِّفْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ قَدْ فَعَلْتُ ‏.‏ قَالَ زِدْ ‏.‏ قَالَ قَدْ فَعَلْتُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ لاَ آمُرُكَ أَنْ تَأْكُلَهَا وَلَوْ شِئْتَ لَمْ تَأْخُذْهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1448" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The dialogue on found property is attributed to Abdullah ibn Umar. Retain his own advice and the repeated questions as supplied.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi that a man found something and went to Abdullah ibn Umar and said to him, &quot;I have found something. What do you think I should do about it?&quot; Abdullah ibn Umar said to him, &quot;Publicise it!&quot; He said, &quot;I have done so.&quot; He said, &quot;Do it again.&quot; He said, &quot;I have done so.&quot; Abdullah said, &quot;I do not order you to use it. If you wished, you could have left it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1448]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1448" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नाफी से रिवायत है के एक शख्स ने लुक्ता पाया, उसको अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास ले आया और पूछा, क्या कहते हो इस बाब में? अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा, लोगों से पूछो और बताओ। उसने कहा, मैं पूछ और बता चुका। अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा, और सही। उसने कहा, मैं पूछ बता चुका हूँ। अब्दुल्लाह ने कहा, मैं कभी तुझ को हुक्म ना करूँगा इसके खाने का, अगर तू चाहता तोह इसको ना लेता।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1448" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne luqta paya, usko Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas le aaya aur pucha, kya kehte ho is baab mein? Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha, logon se pucho aur batao. Usne kaha, main puch aur bata chuka. Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha, aur sahi. Usne kaha, main puch bata chuka hoon. Abdullah ne kaha, main kabhi tujh ko hukum na karunga iske khane ka, agar tu chahta toh isko na leta.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1448" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1434

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1449)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1449]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1449</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1449 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ ثَابِتَ بْنَ الضَّحَّاكِ الأَنْصَارِيَّ، أَخْبَرَهُ ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ، وَجَدَ بَعِيرًا بِالْحَرَّةِ فَعَقَلَهُ ثُمَّ ذَكَرَهُ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَأَمَرَهُ عُمَرُ أَنْ يُعَرِّفَهُ ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ فَقَالَ لَهُ ثَابِتٌ إِنَّهُ قَدْ شَغَلَنِي عَنْ ضَيْعَتِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ أَرْسِلْهُ حَيْثُ وَجَدْتَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Select the transmitted Thabit and Umar camel report following the compiler&#x27;s discussion of a slave&#x27;s liability. That preceding legal discussion is preserved in Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Thabit ibn ad-Dahhak al-Ansari told him that he had found a camel at Harra, so he hobbled it and mentioned it to Umar ibn al-Khattab and Umar ordered him to make it known three times. Thabit said to him, &quot;That would distract me from the running of my estate.&quot; Umar said to him, &quot;Then let it go where you found it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1449]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1449</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1449 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about a slave who finds something and uses it before the term which is set for finds has been reached, and that is a year, is that it is against his person. Either his master gives the price of what his slave has used, or he surrenders his slave to them as compensation. If he withheld it until the term was reached which is set for finds and he used it, it is a debt against him which follows him and it is not against his person and there is nothing against his master in it.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1449 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के साबित बिन दह्हाक अंसारी ने एक ऊंट पाया हर्रा में, उसको रस्सी से बांधा और हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से बयान किया। हज़रत उमर ने कहा, तीन मर्तबा इसको बताओ। साबित ने कहा, अपनी ज़मीन की ख़बर लेने से मैं मजबूर हो गया। हज़रत उमर ने कहा, जहां से तू ने इस ऊंट को पाया है वहीं छोड़ दे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Thabit bin Dhahhak Ansari ne ek oonth paya Harra mein, usko rassi se bandha aur Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se bayan kiya. Hazrat Umar ne kaha, teen martaba isko batao. Thabit ne kaha, apni zameen ki khabar lene se main majboor ho gaya. Hazrat Umar ne kaha, jahan se tu ne is oonth ko paya hai wahin chhod de.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1449" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ وَهُوَ مُسْنِدٌ ظَهْرَهُ إِلَى الْكَعْبَةِ مَنْ أَخَذَ ضَالَّةً فَهُوَ ضَالٌّ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The warning about taking a stray is Umar&#x27;s attributed saying in its reported setting; it is not relabelled as a Prophetic quotation.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that Umar ibn al-Khattab said while he was leaning his back against the Kaba, &quot;Whoever takes a stray is astray</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1450]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1450" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1436

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1451)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ، يَقُولُ كَانَتْ ضَوَالُّ الإِبِلِ فِي زَمَانِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ إِبِلاً مُؤَبَّلَةً تَنَاتَجُ لاَ يَمَسُّهَا أَحَدٌ حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ زَمَانُ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ عَفَّانَ أَمَرَ بِتَعْرِيفِهَا ثُمَّ تُبَاعُ فَإِذَا جَاءَ صَاحِبُهَا أُعْطِيَ ثَمَنَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1451" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). Ibn Shihab reports differing treatment of stray camels under Umar and Uthman. Preserve this historical account under its source narrator, without Prophetic attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he heard Ibn Shihab say, &quot;The stray camels in the time of Umar ibn al-Khattab were numerous and left alone. No one touched them until the time of Uthman ibn Affan. He ordered that they be publicised and then sold, and if the owner came afterwards, he was given their price</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1451]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1451" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शिहाब कहते थे के हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के ज़माने में जो ऊंट (camel) गुम हुए मिलते थे वो छोड़ दिए जाते थे, बच्चे चरा करते थे और कोई उन्हें नहीं लेता था। जब हज़रत उस्मान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) का ज़माना आया तो उन्होंने हुक्म दिया के बताए जाएं, फिर बेच कर उनकी कीमत बैत-उल-माल में रखी जाए, जब मालिक आए तो उसको दे दी जाए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1451" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shihab kehte the ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke zamane mein jo oont (camel) gum huye milte the wo chhod diye jate the, bachche jana karte the aur koi unhe nahi leta tha. Jab Hazrat Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) ka zamana aaya to unhone hukm diya ke bataye jayein, phir bech kar unki keemat Bait-ul-Maal mein rakhi jaye, jab maalik aaye to usko dedi jaye.</p>

## Narration 1437

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1452)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1452]</p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُرَحْبِيلَ بْنِ سَعِيدِ بْنِ سَعْدِ بْنِ عُبَادَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَدِّهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجَ سَعْدُ بْنُ عُبَادَةَ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي بَعْضِ مَغَازِيهِ فَحَضَرَتْ أُمَّهُ الْوَفَاةُ بِالْمَدِينَةِ فَقِيلَ لَهَا أَوْصِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ فِيمَ أُوصِي إِنَّمَا الْمَالُ مَالُ سَعْدٍ ‏.‏ فَتُوُفِّيَتْ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْدَمَ سَعْدٌ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَ سَعْدُ بْنُ عُبَادَةَ ذُكِرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1452" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah answering Sad ibn Ubada about charity for his deceased mother. The full field supplies the mothers final exchange, Sads return, inquiry, the Messengers answer and the garden donation.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Said ibn Amr Shurahbil ibn Said ibn Sad ibn Ubada from his father that his father said, &#x27;&#x27;Sad ibn Ubada went out with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, in one of his raids and his mother was dying in Madina. Someone said to her, &#x27;Leave a testament.&#x27; She said, &#x27;In what shall I leave a testament? The property is Sad&#x27;s property.&#x27; Then she died before Sad returned. When Sad ibn Ubada returned, that was mentioned to him. Sad said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! Will it help her if I give sadaqa for her?&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Yes&#x27; Sad said, &#x27;Such-and-such a garden is sadaqa for her,&#x27; naming the garden</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1452]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1452" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">शरजील बिन सईद बिन साद बिन उबादा से रिवायत है के साद बिन उबादा, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ जिहाद पर निकले, उनकी मां मदीना में मरने लगी। लोगों ने उनसे कहा वसीयत करो, उन्होंने कहा क्या वसीयत करूं, माल तो साद का है, फिर वो मर गई साद के आने से पहले। जब साद आए और लोगों ने बयान किया, साद ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा के अगर मैं अपनी मां की तरफ से लिल्लाह (सदका) दूं तो क्या उसको फायदा होगा? रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया हां। फिर साद ने कहा फलां फलां बाग सदका है मेरी मां की तरफ से।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1452" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="rtl">شرجیل بن سعید بن سعد بن عبادہ سے روایت ہے کہ سعد بن عبادہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ جہاد کر نکلے ان کی ماں مدینہ میں مرنے لگیں لوگوں نے ان سے کہا وصیت کروں انہوں نے کہا کیا وصیت کروں مال تو سعد کا ہے پھر مر گئیں سعد کے آنے سے پہلے۔ جب سعد آئے لوگوں نے بیان کیا سعد نے رسول سے پوچھا کہ اگر میں اپنی ماں کی طرف سے لللہ دوں تو اس کو فائدہ ہوگا۔ رسول اللہ نے فرمایا ہاں پھر سعد نے کہا فلاں فلاں باغ صدقہ ہے میرے ماں کی طرف سے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sharjeel bin Saeed bin Saad bin Ubada se riwayat hai ke Saad bin Ubada, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath jihad par nikle, unki maa Madina mein marne lagi. Logon ne unse kaha wasiyat karo, unhone kaha kya wasiyat karoon, maal to Saad ka hai, phir wo mar gayi Saad ke aane se pehle. Jab Saad aaye aur logon ne bayan kiya, Saad ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha ke agar main apni maa ki taraf se lillah (sadaqa) doon to kya usko fayda hoga? Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya haan. Phir Saad ne kaha falaan falaan bagh sadaqa hai meri maa ki taraf se.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1452" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1438

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1453)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَجُلاً قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِنَّ أُمِّي افْتُلِتَتْ نَفْسُهَا وَأُرَاهَا لَوْ تَكَلَّمَتْ تَصَدَّقَتْ أَفَأَتَصَدَّقُ عَنْهَا فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1453</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1453 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father from A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that a man said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;My mother died suddenly, and I think that had she spoken, she would have given sadaqa. Shall I give sadaqa for her?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Yes</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1453</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1453 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने हिशाम इब्न उरवा से, उन्होंने अपने वालिद से, उन्होंने आइशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत की के एक शख्श ने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा: मेरी वालिदा अचानक फौत हो गईं, मेरा ख्याल है के अगर वो बोलतिं तो सदका करतीं, क्या मैं उन की तरफ से सदका कर सकता हूं? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: हां।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے ہشام بن عروہ سے، انہوں نے اپنے والد سے، انہوں نے عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت کی کہ ایک شخص نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا: میری والدہ اچانک فوت ہو گئیں، میرا خیال ہے کہ اگر وہ بولتیں تو صدقہ کرتیں، کیا میں ان کی طرف سے صدقہ کر سکتا ہوں؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: ہاں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Hisham ibn Urwa se, unhon ne apne walid se, unhon ne A&#x27;isha (R.A) se riwayat ki ke ek shakhs ne Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha: Meri walida achanak fot ho gayin, mera khayal hai ke agar wo boltin to sadaqa kartin, kya main un ki taraf se sadaqa kar sakta hoon? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Haan.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

## Narration 1439

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1454)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ مِنْ بَنِي الْحَارِثِ بْنِ الْخَزْرَجِ تَصَدَّقَ عَلَى أَبَوَيْهِ بِصَدَقَةٍ فَهَلَكَا فَوَرِثَ ابْنُهُمَا الْمَالَ وَهُوَ نَخْلٌ فَسَأَلَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ أُجِرْتَ فِي صَدَقَتِكَ وَخُذْهَا بِمِيرَاثِكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1454</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he heard that a man of the Ansar from the tribe of Banu al-Harith ibn al-Khazraj, gave sadaqa to his parents and then they died. Their son inherited the property he had given them and it was palm-trees. He asked the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, about it and he said, &quot;You are rewarded for your sadaqa, and take it as your inheritance</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आइशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के एक शख़्स ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कहा के मेरी माँ का दम यकायक निकल गया, अगर बात करने पाती तो ज़रूर सदक़ा करती, क्या मैं उसकी तरफ़ से सदक़ा करूँ? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया हाँ। एक अंसारी शख़्स ने अपने वालिदैन को खजूर के दरख़्त सदक़ा में दिए, फिर वालिदैन मर गए तो वही शख़्स उसका वारिस हुआ। उसने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से बयान किया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया तुझे सदक़े का सवाब हुआ, अब मीरास में इसको ले ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہا کہ میرے ماں کا دم یکایک نکل گیا اگر بات کرنے پاتی تو ضرور صدقہ کرتی کیا میں اس کی طرف سے صدقہ کروں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ہاں ۔ ایک شخص انصاری نے اپنے والدین کو کھجور کے درخت صدقہ میں دئیے پھر والدین مر گئے تو وہی شخص اس کا وارث ہو اس نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اتجھے صدقہ کا ثواب ہوا اب میراث میں اس کو لے لے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se kaha ke meri maa ka dum yakayak nikal gaya, agar baat karne paati to zaroor sadaqa karti, kya main uski taraf se sadaqa karoon? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya haan. Ek Ansari shakhs ne apne walidain ko khajoor ke darakht sadaqa mein diye, phir walidain mar gaye to wahi shakhs uska waris hua. Usne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se bayan kiya, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya tujhe sadaqe ka sawab hua, ab miras mein isko le le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1440

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1455)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 37</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَا حَقُّ امْرِئٍ مُسْلِمٍ لَهُ شَىْءٌ يُوصَى فِيهِ يَبِيتُ لَيْلَتَيْنِ إِلاَّ وَوَصِيَّتُهُ عِنْدَهُ مَكْتُوبَةٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1455</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1455 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It is the duty of a muslim man who has something to be given as a bequest not to spend two nights without writing a will about it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community is that when the testator writes something in health or illness as a bequest, and it has freeing slaves or things other than that in it, he can alter it in any way he chooses, until he is on his deathbed. If he prefers to abandon a bequest or change it, he can do so unless he has made a slave mudabbar (to be freed after his death). If he has made him mudabbar, there is no way to change what he has made mudabbar. He is allowed to change his testament because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It is the duty of a muslim man who has something to be given as a bequest not to spend two nights without writing a will about it.&quot; Malik explained, &quot;Had the testator not been able to change his will nor what was mentioned in it about freeing slaves, each testator might withhold making bequests from his property, whether in freeing slaves or other than it. A man gives a bequest in his health and in his travelling.&quot; (i.e. he does not wait till his death bed ) . Malik summed up, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute is that he can change whatever he likes of that except for the mudabbar</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1455</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1455 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया है आदमी को जिसके पास चीज़ या मामला ऐसा हो जिसमें वसीयत करना ज़रूरी हो और वो दो रातें गुज़ारे बग़ैर वसीयत लिखे हुए. मलिक ने कहा अगर मौसी अपनी वसीयत के बदलने पर क़ादिर न होता तो चाहिए था के हर वसीयत करने वाले का माल उसके इख़्तियार से निकल कर रुका रहता तदबीर के. मलिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक हर वसीयत को बदल सकता है सिवाए तद्बीर के.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا نہیں لائق ہے آدمی کو جس کے پاس چیز یا معاملہ ایسا ہو جس میں وصیت کرنا ضروری ہو اور وہ دو راتیں گزارے بغیر وصیت لکھے ہؤے کہا مالک نے اگر موصی اپنی وصیت کے بدلنے پر قادر نہ ہوتا تو چاہیے تھا کہ ہر وصیت کرنے والے کا مال اس کے اختیار سے نکل کر رکا رہتا حالانکہ ایسا نہیں ہے کبھی آدمی اپنی صحت کو بدل سکتا ہے سوائے تدبیر کے۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک ہر وصیت کو بدل سکتا ہے سائے تدبیر کے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, nahi layak hai aadmi ko jiske paas cheez ya mamla aisa ho jisme wasiyat karna zaroori ho aur wo do raatein guzare baghair wasiyat likhe huye. Malik ne kaha agar mosi apni wasiyat ke badalne par qadir na hota to chahiye tha ke har wasiyat karne wale ka maal uske ikhtiyar se nikal kar ruka rehta, halanki aisa nahi hai, kabhi aadmi apni sehat ko badal sakta hai siwaye tadbeer ke. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek har wasiyat ko badal sakta hai siwaye tadbeer ke.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1441

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 37</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1456]</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1456</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1456 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ عَمْرَو بْنَ سُلَيْمٍ الزُّرَقِيَّ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، قِيلَ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ إِنَّ هَا هُنَا غُلاَمًا يَفَاعًا لَمْ يَحْتَلِمْ مِنْ غَسَّانَ وَوَارِثُهُ بِالشَّامِ وَهُوَ ذُو مَالٍ وَلَيْسَ لَهُ هَا هُنَا إِلاَّ ابْنَةُ عَمٍّ لَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فَلْيُوصِ لَهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ فَأَوْصَى لَهَا بِمَالٍ يُقَالُ لَهُ بِئْرُ جُشَمٍ قَالَ عَمْرُو بْنُ سُلَيْمٍ فَبِيعَ ذَلِكَ الْمَالُ بِثَلاَثِينَ أَلْفَ دِرْهَمٍ وَابْنَةُ عَمِّهِ الَّتِي أَوْصَى لَهَا هِيَ أُمُّ عَمْرِو بْنِ سُلَيْمٍ الزُّرَقِيِّ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1456" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Select the reported request and Umar&#x27;s permission for the adolescent&#x27;s bequest. Malik&#x27;s later identification and sale-price annotation remains commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Hazm that Amr ibn Sulaym az-Zuraqi informed his father that it had been said to Umar ibn al-Khattab, &quot;There is here an adolescent boy who has not yet reached puberty. He is from the Ghassan tribe and his heir is in ash- Sham. He has property. Here he only has the daughter of one of his paternal uncles.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab instructed, &quot;Let him leave her a bequest.&quot; He willed her a property called the well of Jusham.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1456]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1456" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1456</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1456 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik added, &quot;That property was sold for 30,000 dirhams, and the daughter of the paternal uncle to whom he willed it was the mother of Amr ibn Sulaym az-Zuraqi</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1456" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने अब्दुल्लाह इब्न अबी बक्र इब्न हज़्म से रिवायत की के उमर इब्न अल-ख़त्ताब (र.अ) को बताया गया के एक नाबालिग़ लड़का है जिस का कोई क़रीबी वारिस नहीं, सिर्फ़ चाचा ज़ाद बहन है, उमर (र.अ) ने फ़रमाया: उसे वसीयत करने दो। चुनांचे उस ने उसे एक कुँआ वसीयत कर दिया जो बाद में तीस हज़ार दिरहम का फ़रोख़्त हुआ।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1456" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1457 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَنَّ غُلاَمًا، مِنْ غَسَّانَ حَضَرَتْهُ الْوَفَاةُ بِالْمَدِينَةِ وَوَارِثُهُ بِالشَّامِ فَذُكِرَ ذَلِكَ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقِيلَ لَهُ إِنَّ فُلاَنًا يَمُوتُ أَفَيُوصِي قَالَ فَلْيُوصِ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ قَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ وَكَانَ الْغُلاَمُ ابْنَ عَشْرِ سِنِينَ أَوِ اثْنَتَىْ عَشْرَةَ سَنَةً ‏.‏ قَالَ فَأَوْصَى بِبِئْرِ جُشَمٍ فَبَاعَهَا أَهْلُهَا بِثَلاَثِينَ أَلْفَ دِرْهَمٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الضَّعِيفَ فِي عَقْلِهِ وَالسَّفِيهَ وَالْمُصَابَ الَّذِي يُفِيقُ أَحْيَانًا تَجُوزُ وَصَايَاهُمْ إِذَا كَانَ مَعَهُمْ مِنْ عُقُولِهِمْ مَا يَعْرِفُونَ مَا يُوصُونَ بِهِ فَأَمَّا مَنْ لَيْسَ مَعَهُ مِنْ عَقْلِهِ مَا يَعْرِفُ بِذَلِكَ مَا يُوصِي بِهِ وَكَانَ مَغْلُوبًا عَلَى عَقْلِهِ فَلاَ وَصِيَّةَ لَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Preserve the narrated bequest case, Umar&#x27;s response and the transmitted age and sale details. Exclude the following general legal explanation attributed to Malik from the narration quotation.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Abu Bakr ibn Hazm that a boy from Ghassan was dying in Madina while his heir was in Syria. That was mentioned to Umar ibn al-Khattab. It was said to him, &quot;So-and-so is dying. Shall he make a bequest?&quot; He said, &quot;Let him make a bequest.&quot; Yahya ibn Said said that Abu Bakr had said, &quot;He was a boy of ten or twelve years.&quot; Yahya said, &quot;He willed the well of Jusham, and his people sold it for 30,000 dirhams.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1457]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1457</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1457 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community is that a simpleton, an idiot, or a lunatic who recovers at times, can make wills if they have enough of their wits about them to recognise what they will. Someone who has not enough wits to recognise what he wills, and is overcome in his intellect, cannot make a bequest</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1457]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1457</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1457 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन सालिम ज़ुरक़ी से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से कहा गया के इस जगह मदीना में एक लड़का है क़रीब-ए-बुलूग़ के मगर बालिग़ नहीं हुआ, क़बीला ग़स्सान से और उसके वारिस शाम में हैं और उसके पास माल है और यहाँ उसका कोई वारिस नहीं सिवाए एक चाचा ज़ाद बहन के। तो हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा उसको वसीयत करे। उस लड़के ने माल की वसीयत जिसका नाम बीर जशम था अपनी चाचा ज़ाद बहन के वास्ते की। अम्र बिन सालिम ने कहा वो माल तीस हज़ार दिरहम का बिका और उसकी चाचा ज़ाद बहन अम्र बिन सालिम की माँ थी। अबू बक्र बिन हज़्म से रिवायत है के एक लड़का ग़स्सान का मरने लगा मदीना में और वारिस उसके शाम में थे, हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से उसका ज़िक्र हुआ और पूछा गया क्या वसीयत करे? आप ने फ़रमाया वसीयत करे। यहया बिन सईद ने कहा वो लड़का दस बरस का था या बारह बरस का और बीर जशम छोड़ गया, उसकी वसीयत कर गया, लोगों ने उसे तीस हज़ार दिरहम का बेचा। मालिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक ये हुक्म इत्तेफ़ाक़ी है के ज़ईफ़-उल-अक़्ल और नादान और मजनून की जिसको कभी अफ़ाक़ा हो जाता है वसीयत दुरुस्त है, जब इतनी अक़्ल रखते हों के वसीयत जो करें उसको समझें, अगर इतनी भी अक़्ल न हो तो उसकी वसीयत दुरुस्त नहीं है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عمرو بن سلیم زرقی سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمربن خطاب سے کہا گیا کہ اس جگہ مدینہ میں ایک لڑکا ہے قریب بلوغ کے مگر بالغ نہیں ہوا قبیلہ غسان سے اور اس کے وارث شام میں ہیں اور اس کے پاس مال ہے اور یہاں اس کا کوئی وارث نہیں سوائے ایک چچا زاد بہن کے تو حضرت عمر نے کہا اس کو وصیت کرے اس لڑکے نے مال کی وصیت جس کا نام بیر جشم تھا اپنی چچا زاد بہن کے واسطے کی عمرو بن سلیم نے کہا وہ مال تیس ہزار درہم کا بکا اور اس کی چچا زاد بہن عمرو بن سلیم کی ماں تھی ۔ ابوبکر بن حزم سے روایت ہے کہ ایک لڑکا غسان کا مرنے لگا مدینہ میں اور وارث اس کے شام میں تھے حضرت عمر سے اس کا ذکر ہوا اور پوچھا گیا کیا وصیت کرے آپ نے فرمایا وصیت کرے یحیی بن سعید نے کہا وہ لڑکا دس برس کا تھا یا بارہ برس کا اور بیر جشم چھوڑ گیا اس کی وصیت کر گیا لوگوں نے اسے تیس ہزار درہم کا بیچا۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اتفاقی ہے کہ ضعیف العقل اور نادان اور مجنوں کی جس کو کبھی آفاقہ ہوجاتا ہے وصیت درست ہے جب اتنی عقل رکھتے ہوں کہ وصیت جو کریں اس کو سمجھیں اگر اتنی بھی عقل نہ ہو تو اس کی وصیت درست نہیں ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1457</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1457 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Amr bin Salim Zurqi se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) se kaha gaya ke is jagah Madina mein ek ladka hai qareeb-e-bulugh ke magar baligh nahi hua, qabeela Ghassan se aur uske waris Shaam mein hain aur uske paas maal hai aur yahan uska koi waris nahi siwaye ek chacha zad behen ke. To Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha usko wasiyat kare. Us ladke ne maal ki wasiyat jiska naam Bir Jashm tha apni chacha zad behen ke waste ki. Amr bin Salim ne kaha wo maal tees hazaar dirham ka bika aur uski chacha zad behen Amr bin Salim ki maa thi. Abu Bakr bin Hazm se riwayat hai ke ek ladka Ghassan ka marne laga Madina mein aur waris uske Shaam mein the, Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se uska zikr hua aur pucha gaya kya wasiyat kare? Aap ne farmaya wasiyat kare. Yahya bin Saeed ne kaha wo ladka das baras ka tha ya barah baras ka aur Bir Jashm chhod gaya, uski wasiyat kar gaya, logon ne use tees hazaar dirham ka becha. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek ye hukm ittefaqi hai ke zaeef-ul-aql aur nadan aur majnoon ki jisko kabhi afaqa ho jata hai wasiyat durust hai, jab itni aql rakhte hon ke wasiyat jo karein usko samjhein, agar itni bhi aql na ho to uski wasiyat durust nahi hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1457" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1443

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1458)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 37</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَامِرِ بْنِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَنِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَعُودُنِي عَامَ حَجَّةِ الْوَدَاعِ مِنْ وَجَعٍ اشْتَدَّ بِي فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَدْ بَلَغَ بِي مِنَ الْوَجَعِ مَا تَرَى وَأَنَا ذُو مَالٍ وَلاَ يَرِثُنِي إِلاَّ ابْنَةٌ لِي أَفَأَتَصَدَّقُ بِثُلُثَىْ مَالِي قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ لاَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ فَالشَّطْرُ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ الثُّلُثُ وَالثُّلُثُ كَثِيرٌ إِنَّكَ أَنْ تَذَرَ وَرَثَتَكَ أَغْنِيَاءَ خَيْرٌ مِنْ أَنْ تَذَرَهُمْ عَالَةً يَتَكَفَّفُونَ النَّاسَ وَإِنَّكَ لَنْ تُنْفِقَ نَفَقَةً تَبْتَغِي بِهَا وَجْهَ اللَّهِ إِلاَّ أُجِرْتَ حَتَّى مَا تَجْعَلُ فِي فِي امْرَأَتِكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَأُخَلَّفُ بَعْدَ أَصْحَابِي فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّكَ لَنْ تُخَلَّفَ فَتَعْمَلَ عَمَلاً صَالِحًا إِلاَّ ازْدَدْتَ بِهِ دَرَجَةً وَرِفْعَةً وَلَعَلَّكَ أَنْ تُخَلَّفَ حَتَّى يَنْتَفِعَ بِكَ أَقْوَامٌ وَيُضَرَّ بِكَ آخَرُونَ اللَّهُمَّ أَمْضِ لأَصْحَابِي هِجْرَتَهُمْ وَلاَ تَرُدَّهُمْ عَلَى أَعْقَابِهِمْ لَكِنِ الْبَائِسُ سَعْدُ ابْنُ خَوْلَةَ يَرْثِي لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ مَاتَ بِمَكَّةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1458]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1458" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
